# InfiniBot third-party licenses and attributions

Generated from the read-only integration tree at commit `e5c71ee2cc4a4d246285160a1c0f3b55abb7943a`.

This inventory covers resolved production npm/pnpm and Python components plus the distributed vendored/model/container/font/media groups found in the repository. Package authorship fields are taken from package metadata or a shipped license file; where neither declares a holder, that gap is stated rather than guessed. License alternatives are preserved. A permissive branch may be selected where an expression uses `OR`; every term applies where it uses `AND`.

### GSAP Standard ‘No Charge’ License

The npm package declares only the URL below and does not ship a license file. Preserve the package’s proprietary notices and review the authoritative terms directly before any distribution:

- https://gsap.com/community/standard-license/

This custom license permits ordinary commercial projects but restricts competitive visual-animation builders, omits a general sublicense grant, and is not an OSI open-source license. InfiniBot should replace GSAP or obtain explicit redistribution/OEM rights before an FSL source release.

### Gemma Terms

EmbeddingGemma is not bundled in the inspected tree. Its custom terms govern any user/operator download and hosted use:

- https://ai.google.dev/gemma/terms

- https://ai.google.dev/gemma/prohibited_use_policy

### Lessac Blizzard 2013 research license

The referenced Piper amy/lessac voices inherit a provenance problem from research-only source material. The authoritative agreement is:

- https://www.cstr.ed.ac.uk/projects/blizzard/2013/lessac_blizzard2013/license.html

It excludes commercial voice-synthesis products/services; those voice packs must not be used in the hosted commercial product absent separate written rights.
